- **nano-pdf**: Work with PDF files using Python + PyPDF2. ALWAYS pip install PyPDF2 first. Use PdfReader to extract text (page.extract_text()), PdfMerger to merge PDFs. Page count = len(reader.pages). CRITICAL: when extracting author names or titles from PDFs, NEVER use PDF metadata (it often says 'anonymous'/'untitled'). Instead extract text from page 1 and parse it: title is the first line, author is on a line containing 'Author:'. For money values remove $ and commas, for percentages remove %. Output JSON with json.dump(data, f, indent=2). Numbers must be int/float not strings. ([lec77/skillvm-data](https://github.com/lec77/skillvm-data/tree/HEAD/skill-variants/targeted/bare/qwen3.5-122b/nano-pdf))
